Create /docs/mvp/demo-readiness.md and make your demo repeatable.

    Step-by-step demo script (exact clicks/requests)
    Known issues (what might break and how you’ll handle it)
    Seed data plan (how you ensure consistent demo data)
    Fallback plan (what you do if environment fails)
    Timebox: target a 5–7 minute MVP demo path

Expectation: Another teammate should be able to run your demo from this doc without guessing.

**cloud-kimi-k2**:  Here's my **honest difficulty assessment** based on where teams typically struggle:

## **Overall Difficulty: 🔴 High**

This is a **"prove it works" week** — heavier than Week 3 because you're building under constraint, not just documenting.

---

## **Per-Deliverable Breakdown**

### **A) MVP Progress Evidence** 
**Difficulty: 🟢 Easy (if honest) / 🔴 Hard (if BSing)**

- Mechanical task: Update markdown, link board
- **Trap**: Teams often claim stories are "Done" that aren't merged. The rubric says **"Done means merged to main with checks"** — if you haven't merged, you get zero on B
- Time: 1-2 hours if your board is accurate

---

### **B) Core MVP Stories (2 end-to-end)** 
**Difficulty: 🔴🔴 Very High — This is the killer**

- **40 points** = The meat of the grade
- **"End-to-end"** usually means: UI → API → Database → Backend logic → Response
- **Perils**:
  - Story 1 is easy, Story 2 hits integration hell
  - "Review approval" requires teammates to actually review (bottleneck risk)
  - One broken dependency and your "2 stories" becomes "1.5 stories"
  - "README still accurate" — docs drift from code constantly

**Real talk**: If your Week 6 scope lock was ambitious, you're in crunch mode. Two *thin* vertical slices beat two *horizontal* layers.

**Time estimate**: 15-25 hours team effort (coding, review, fixing, merging)

---

### **C) Tests for Demo Path (4 tests)** 
**Difficulty: 🟡 Medium-High**

- The **failure path test** is where people trip — requires thinking about validation, auth, missing data, error states
- **Boundary test** requires diving into service/data layer (not just UI)
- **CI integration** — if your tests pass locally but fail in CI (env vars, DB setup), debugging eats hours
- **Flaky test trap**: Writing tests that pass today but fail tomorrow = no points

**Time**: 4-6 hours if your app is testable. 12+ hours if you haven't set up test infrastructure yet.

---

### **D) Demo Readiness** 
**Difficulty: 🟡 Medium (tedious + coordination)**

- Easy to write vague docs, **hard** to make it "another teammate can run it blind"
- **Seed data plan** — if your demo depends on specific records existing, you need scripts/migrations
- **Known issues** requires admitting what's broken (psychologically hard)
- **Fallback plan** — teams skip this, then panic when WiFi dies during demo

**Time**: 3-4 hours of actual rehearsal + documenting, not just typing

---

## **Risk Hotspots** 🚨

| Risk | Why It Hurts | Mitigation |
|------|-------------|------------|
| **Story dependencies** | Story B requires Story A merged first | Pick two **independent** stories, not sequential ones |
| **Review bottleneck** | Teammate busy, PR sits for 2 days | Start reviews by Wednesday latest |
| **"Almost done" stories** | Code works locally, not in CI | Merge early, fix in follow-up (but meets "merged to main" rule) |
| **Test coverage vanity** | Writing 4 easy tests vs. required mix | Map explicitly: 2 happy + 1 fail + 1 boundary |
| **Demo data rot** | Records get deleted, demo breaks Tuesday | Write seed script in `/scripts/seed-demo.js` |

---

**Hardest → Easiest ranking:**
1. **B** (start immediately — this determines your grade)
2. **C** (do in parallel as you code B — don't tack on Friday)
3. **D** (do Thursday after B is merged — needs real working code to document)
4. **A** (fill in Friday — just reflects reality of B, C, D)

**Confidence check**: If you don't have 1 story merged by **Wednesday night**, you're behind. Cut scope immediately.

## 1. Pre-Demo Setup (Run This 10 Minutes Before)

### A. Environment Check
```bash
# 1. Verify deployment is live
curl https://your-app.com/health || echo "ALERT: Deployed env down"

# 2. Verify local backup works (if deployed env fails)
npm run dev:demo  # Uses demo database, not prod
```

### B. Seed Data Injection (Required for consistent demo)
```bash
# Run this EXACTLY once before demo starts
npm run db:seed:demo

# Expected output: "Seeded 3 demo users, 5 sample records, 1 workflow"
# If output differs, STOP and debug
```

**Demo Data State After Seeding:**
| Entity | Count | Key Details |
|--------|-------|-------------|
| Test Users | 2 | `demo@user.com` / `Password123!` (regular), `admin@demo.com` / `Admin123!` |
| Sample Records | 5 | IDs: 1001-1005, all "Active" status |
| Workflow Templates | 1 | "Standard Onboarding" |

### C. Browser Setup (Avoid demo-killing surprises)
- [ ] Open Incognito/Private window (no cached auth)
- [ ] Zoom: 110% (better for screen sharing)
- [ ] DevTools Console: Visible (shows errors to audience = credibility)
- [ ] Disable notifications (OS level)

---

## 2. Step-by-Step Demo Script

**Narrative Arc:** [One sentence hook — e.g., "Watch a new employee complete onboarding in 90 seconds"]

### Phase 1: Setup (1 min)
| Time | Step | Action | Expected Result | Narration |
|------|------|--------|-----------------|-----------|
| 0:00 | Open app | Navigate to `/` | Login page loads | "Here's our MVP... notice the clean entry point" |
| 0:30 | Login | Enter `demo@user.com` / `Password123!`, click Login | Dashboard loads with 5 active items | "Our demo user starts their day here..." |
| 1:00 | Show context | Click "My Tasks" tab | Task list appears (Task ID 1001-1003) | "These are the priority items seeded for today" |

### Phase 2: Core Flow — [YOUR MVP PATH NAME] (3-4 min)
| Time | Step | Action | Expected Result | Narration |
|------|------|--------|-----------------|-----------|
| 1:30 | Start task | Click task "Complete Profile Update" | Detail modal/page opens | "User picks the highest priority task..." |
| 2:00 | Execute | Fill form: Name="Demo User", Role="Analyst", Save | Success toast, status changes to "Complete" | "Notice the validation working... [explain logic briefly]" |
| 2:30 | Verify | Return to dashboard | Dashboard shows "4 active, 1 completed" | "System updates in real-time..." |
| 3:00 | [Second Story] | [Next action — e.g., "Generate Report"] | [Expected result] | "Now watch the automation trigger..." |
| 3:30 | [Completion] | [Final action] | [Success state] | "And that's the MVP loop closed." |

### Phase 3: Wrap/QA (1 min)
| Time | Step | Action | Expected Result | Narration |
|------|------|--------|-----------------|-----------|
| 4:00 | Show audit | Click "View History" | Log shows timestamped actions | "Everything is tracked for compliance..." |
| 4:30 | Logout | Click user menu → Logout | Returns to login | "Clean session end" |

**Total Scripted Time:** ~5 minutes  
**Buffer for questions:** 2 minutes

---

## 3. Known Issues (Do Not Pretend These Don't Exist)

| Issue | When It Happens | Severity | Mitigation During Demo |
|-------|-----------------|----------|------------------------|
| [e.g., "Report generation takes 4-5 seconds"] | When clicking "Generate" on task 1002 | Medium | **Pre-generate** during Phase 1 (hidden tab) OR say "This takes a moment in MVP, we're optimizing" |
| [e.g., "Date picker shows wrong timezone in staging"] | If user checks timestamps | Low | Don't click timestamps. If asked: "That's a staging config, fixed in production config" |
| [e.g., "Console shows 404 for favicon.ico"] | Always | Low | **Ignore.** Non-fatal, cosmetic only |

**DO NOT DEMO THESE:** [List features that are broken/unseeded — e.g., "Forgot password link", "Admin panel bulk actions"]

---

## 4. Fallback Plans (When Things Break)

### Scenario A: Deployed Environment Down
**Trigger:** 502 error, login fails 3x
**Action:**
1. "Let me switch to the local backup"
2. Open `http://localhost:3000` (pre-seeded)
3. Continue from Phase 1, Step 3 (skip login, already logged in)
4. Narrate: "Since we're on local, you'll see [difference] but the core flow is identical"

### Scenario B: Data Corruption (Wrong Records Showing)
**Trigger:** Task list shows 0 items or 100+ items
**Action:**
1. "Let me reset to the known good state — one moment"
2. Run `npm run db:reset:demo` behind the scenes (or pause screen share for 10 sec)
3. Refresh browser
4. Resume: "Fresh demo state. As I was saying..."

### Scenario C: Critical Bug Discovered Mid-Demo
**Trigger:** App crashes, form won't submit, 500 error
**Action:**
1. **Acknowledge it:** "We hit a known edge case in the staging environment"
2. **Pivot to screenshot:** Open `/docs/mvp/screenshots/success-state.png` — "Here's the completed state"
3. **OR pivot to code:** "Let me show you the test that verifies this works" (switch to IDE, run test)
4. Timebox: Never spend >30 seconds debugging live

### Scenario D: User Questions Sidetrack Demo
**Trigger:** "But what if [complex scenario]?"
**Action:** "Great question — that's in our Phase 2 scope. For today's MVP focus, let me show you the happy path..."

---

## 5. Post-Demo Reset (For Next Runner)

After demo completes (success or failure):
```bash
# Clean up any data created during demo (optional but recommended)
npm run db:seed:demo -- --clean

# Verify reset worked
curl http://localhost:3000/api/tasks | grep "count.*5" || echo "Reset failed"
```

If another teammate needs to run this:

1. **Pre-reqs:** Node 18+, `.env.demo` file in repo
2. **Setup:** `npm install && npm run db:seed:demo`
3. **Start:** `npm run dev:demo`
4. **Follow:** Section 2 step-by-step (print it out)
5. **If stuck:** Check Section 3 (Known Issues), then Section 4 (Fallbacks)

**Success Criteria for Blind Run:** Teammate can complete demo without asking questions or hitting unrecoverable errors.
